# VBOS Clean-Start MVP

Standalone VBOS Clean mobile app. Source now lives in https://github.com/VahramSargsyan/vbos-clean; legacy VBOS remains independent and unchanged.

- **Public TEST URL:** https://vbos-clean-mvp-2026.onrender.com/
- **Source authority:** vbos-clean/main, extracted from vbos-app feature/vbos-mvp-clean-20261103 @ e17643a0c399d859abe85c4057a7ec732ac25526. New repo import commit: 67a0fe2fabec014160ed90aa876880a67a7b0046.
- **Render service:** srv-dbkvfh2jnfac73a31nv0 (free, Frankfurt, autoDeploy OFF). Connected to this repository main; TEST LIVE deploy dep-dbtbjat6e07s73c0i9k0 at 1cf42345b35ce15f397c5fb1bd59821d5df8f7dc. Owner source-switch smoke accepted 2026-11-16.
- **Database:** existing free Neon project proud-hat-90291191, new isolated database vbos_mvp_clean
- **Data policy:** historical legacy archive imported separately (131 source_records and additional CRM archive), but it does not automatically turn those into writable Clean-native CRM or WorkLog records. See the acceptance/migration register.
- **Legacy finance:** historical archive/read-only views and opening previews exist. Native finance cutover remains BLOCKED with GOOGLE write authority, 0 native transactions. Financial measures must not be silently added together.
- **Auth:** first-launch owner registration with a one-time claim code; owner picks their own login/password; scrypt owner-password hash resides in PostgreSQL, claim code is stored only as SHA256 in Render env, and the cookie HMAC secret resides only in Render env.
- **Postgres credentials:** Render-only DATABASE_URL; not exposed in browser.
- **No PROD authority switch** or accepted legacy runtime replacement was made.
- **Data export:** authenticated Reports > Download JSON. Free service is not a substitute for a physical backup strategy.

## Capability and migration status as of 2026-11-16

Authoritative current TEST status and the remaining user/financial migration gates: [VBOS Clean acceptance & migration register](docs/status/VBOS_CLEAN_ACCEPTANCE_MIGRATION_REGISTER_20261116.md). Historical Build Packets below describe their own original checkpoints and may contain older not-yet-released statements; do not override the current exact release receipt.

## Android installation and owner registration
Open the HTTPS URL once in Chrome on Android, choose **⋮ → Install app** / **Add to Home screen**. The manifest, PNG icons, standalone display and service worker make it installable like Investment Lab. The first-launch screen asks for a self-selected login/password and the one-time activation code. After registration, use the login/password; no automatic access through ChatGPT is claimed. No APK is produced; this is an installable PWA.

## Main flows
CRM: create lead, update status, create client and object; Orders: create/link order and change status; Today: open/close day, start/stop one work session, record performed work with minutes/quantity; Reports: static legacy finance snapshot.
All new records are written to vbos_mvp_clean only. No backend Google calls.

## Repeatable setup
Execute schema.sql against an *empty dedicated* database; do not run it against PROD or any shared legacy schema. Insert the owner-approved legacy report separately. Provide DATABASE_URL, CLAIM_TOKEN_SHA256 (SHA256 hex of a strong random one-time activation code) and COOKIE_KEY (40+ cryptographically random chars) in Render environment. Never commit or publish the plaintext claim code; provide it only to the owner. APP_PASSWORD_HASH is obsolete and ignored.
Build command for NEW repository root: npm install --omit=dev --no-audit --no-fund
Start command for NEW repository root: node server.mjs
Static source-layout check: npm run verify:source (no DB, no Actions). Unit tests: npm test. Disposable-PostgreSQL E2E: npm run test:e2e with isolated TEST_DATABASE_URL. No tests are triggered by adding these commands.

## Rollback
Keep original VBOS untouched. Roll back the Render service to previous exact deployment. Do not delete the new PostgreSQL database to roll back code. Data exports should be captured before any major schema changes.

## Not yet proven
The exact 2026-11-16 source-switch TEST and owner login/existing-record Android smoke were accepted; full business-workflow authenticated write/readback tests for every module remain NOT_RUN. CI has confirmed hosted PWA manifest/icons, owner-bootstrap endpoint, rejected unauthenticated data access, and public DB health; startup verifies real transactional PostgreSQL write/readback/rollback. It is a new small app, not the complete old CRM / Today UI. Verify functionality before recording real important data.

## Functional block V2 — edit + bounded work history (2026-11-03)
- Client: edit name/phone/notes via revision-guarded UPDATE (same client UUID and object relations).
- Object: edit name/address/notes via PostgreSQL row-version token (xmin), without changing object UUID or client/order relations. A stale token fails with HTTP 409; refresh and re-apply the user's edits.
- Work history: authenticated GET /api/history with inclusive from/to, optional orderId, validated limit 1–50, stable keyset cursor and hasMore. The UI defaults to one day, supports a date range and order filtering, loads only a page at a time, displays details and allows correction of date, order, description, minutes, quantity, unit and notes.
- WorkLog corrections are revision-guarded UPDATE on the same UUID; no physical DELETE, no wholesale history load.
- No SQL migration: existing mvp_clients.revision and mvp_work_logs.revision are used; object row-version is PostgreSQL's internal xmin exposed as an ephemeral comparison token. If the database is restored, the old token may become invalid: refresh before editing.
- Scope: only the independent Neon TEST database and existing Render TEST PWA; old VBOS, Google PROD and other test systems untouched.
- Required checks: npm test; npm run test:e2e in GitHub Actions with disposable local PostgreSQL; exact Render commit + authenticated Android acceptance remain separate.
- Rollback: redeploy exact baseline commit 078b15eb84aec99902b217b33c467cad081547d3; API and UI changes use the existing table contracts, so no destructive database rollback is required. Actual edits already made by the owner are not reversed by redeployment.

## Functional block V3 — searchable mobile catalog + lead/order editing (2026-11-03)
- Search all saved leads/clients/objects/orders through authenticated GET /api/catalog, not the capped 500-record dashboard lists. Each request limits rows to 1–50 and supports keyset pagination on created_at/id; a full UUID uses indexed primary-key equality. Query terms 2–80 chars (or empty for recent), parameterized substring search. PostgreSQL is canonical.
- Edit lead title/contact/phone/notes and order title/planned date/notes with revision-guarded updates preserving IDs, links and status. Includes edit controls on original CRM/Orders screens and search results.
- No new tables, indexes or legacy imports; search terms on non-ID fields currently use a database-side bounded response but may scan rows until indexed search is separately authorized.
- New UI tab "Поиск", mobile form, category filter, "show more", safe HTML escaping. No hard delete; edit conflict returns 409 and refresh required.
- Tests: npm test (client UI security/structure) + GitHub CI disposable PostgreSQL E2E for auth, revisions, FK retention, exact-ID, filters, cursor page, validation and wildcard literal.
- Scope: same isolated TEST Render+Neon. No authenticated Android acceptance until owner chooses to test. Rollback: redeploy exact V2 commit 84844ef31e0d43f06ef4e4723858c0e960322e51; no data-migration rollback.

It projects current day state, current active session joined to order/object/client, up to 20 today's sessions, 10 today's detailed logs plus accurate full-day totals, 25 active/today planned orders, and summaries for the 7 latest opened workdays. The UI marks truncated lists and links into existing WorkLog history.

## V6 — independent Daily A1 in PostgreSQL TEST
- Based on accepted legacy DailyA1Service_v1_4_0_3.js and DailyA1MyWorkUi_v1_4_0_3.html: one A1 per Asia/Yerevan day, stable ID `DA1-yyyy-mm-dd`, goal/reason/notes, status open/completed, result COMPLETED/PARTIAL/NOT_COMPLETED and outcome note; reopen clears the outcome but does not alter WorkDay or WorkLog.
- New dedicated `mvp_daily_a1` table and `mvp_a1_requests` replay receipts. Versioned additive migration: `migrations/0001_daily_a1.sql`. **Only execute in new isolated `vbos_mvp_clean` TEST database after disposable PostgreSQL CI rehearsal.** A1 does not import Google history and is not sourced from legacy WorkDay.
- Security: owner-authenticated `GET /api/a1`, guarded POST `/api/action` types `a1_save`, `a1_complete`, `a1_reopen`. Request ID and payload hash transactionally deduplicate retries. Revision protects against stale changes. Invalid date/outcome fails without mutation.
- UI: Today and My Work share A1 component; no separate duplicate state. Physical presence, worker days and business time remain NOT_IMPLEMENTED and must not be inferred from A1.
- Rollback: redeploy V5 exact `215d5f4562bdbaa4d5415f2d0df4403a7c4f63bd` / `dep-dbl1pl6k1f9s73e9d760`; **leave additive A1 tables and owner records intact**. Destructive teardown not authorized. See `migrations/0001_daily_a1_ROLLBACK.md`.

## V7 — object presence independent of WorkDay/WorkLog
- Based on accepted legacy `WorkSessionService_v1_4_0_8.js` and `MyWorkObjectSessions_v1_4_0_17.html`: per-object arrival and departure, no overlapping visits, one open object, closed-session time corrections and date-selected history.
- New versioned additive TEST-only `migrations/0002_object_presence.sql`: `mvp_object_presence` and `mvp_presence_requests`; UUID/FK to existing workday, object and optional order, status open/closed, timestamps, revision, transactional idempotency receipts. No existing table/ID/status/relation migration.
- Adaptation for new standalone MVP with objects but no orders: a visit can be linked directly to an object with optional order; if supplied order must belong to that object and cannot be done/cancelled.
- User first opens a workday; then may record presence. Closing a day is prohibited until all visits and work intervals end. Presence does not automatically start/stop a work session, create a WorkLog or adjust A1/owner business time.
- Owner-authenticated `GET /api/presence?date=YYYY-MM-DD&cursor=...` paginates 25 at a time; `presence_start`, `presence_stop`, `presence_update` use guarded atomic PostgreSQL and optimistic revision. Users can correct recorded arrival/departure times and notes but not rewrite the original object or day IDs. The authenticated JSON export includes up to 500 presence visits and explicitly rejects truncated exports.
- Existing TEST service only. CI disposable PostgreSQL rehearses migration twice and tests auth/duplicate/overlap, correction, day-close gate, paging and export. Rollback code to V6 exact `25fa232e68a2af91567cafe1ad3142d9ffaf8d73` / Render `dep-dbl23bei0phs73bjpr0g`; leave additive presence data/tables intact. Physical Android acceptance deferred.

## V8 — independent owner business time (2026-11-03)
- Source contract from legacy `OwnerActivityService.js` and `OwnerActivityTimezoneHotfix_v1_4_0_2.js`: two distinct classes ORGANIZATION/«Организация» and BUSINESS_SYSTEM/«Компания / система»; one active timer; start/stop; summary, notes, historical corrections, archive; Asia/Yerevan wall clock and split at local midnight.
- Versioned isolated TEST-only migration `migrations/0003_owner_activity.sql`: new `mvp_owner_activities` and `mvp_owner_activity_requests`, unique partial active index, exact date/end boundary checks, revision and request UUID replay receipts. No legacy business-time data import. No existing WorkDay, Object Presence, WorkLog, A1 or owner settings changes.
- Owner-authenticated `GET /api/owner-activity` with bounded date/cursor paging; guarded `owner_activity_start/stop/update/archive` through existing action endpoint. Optional WorkDay FK auto-attaches on matching date but does not start or close it. Optional Lead/Client/Object/WorkOrder link is verified before saving.
- Owner Activity may overlap physical visits or WorkLog; shown totals are **raw recorded minutes**, not unique union time, and must not be used as financial owner-hour yield. Historical dates before first source record are tracking-unavailable, not proven zero.
- Backups: authenticated JSON export includes `ownerActivityRecords`, refuses >500 instead of silently truncating. CI disposable PostgreSQL E2E rehearses migration twice and verifies idempotent replay, archived exclusion, cross-midnight split, optimistic corrections, optional links and independence.
- Rollback to base exact TEST candidate `951fe6ecdc1e9781480cf473793767a16303d3a7` / Render `dep-dbl2k38u01pc73elbtl0`; leave additive new tables and any user data untouched; no destructive rollback authorized.
- UI via My Work on Android, behind existing hidden drawer. Physical owner Android acceptance NOT_RUN by explicit owner preference; no new infrastructure/main/PROD/Google changes.


## V9A — manual ScheduleItems planner (2026-11-04)
- **Source contract:** accepted legacy `ScheduleService.js` / `Schedule.html`. New canonical `public.mvp_schedule_items` records are independent of `mvp_orders.planned_on`, WorkLog, A1, business-time and object visits. Week starts Monday in Asia/Yerevan; month means calendar month. Historical completed/cancelled records persist; archive hides a plan from the calendar but preserves it for export.
- **UI:** new hidden menu «План» and Today shortcut, previous/today/next, week/month, mobile daily selection, creation/edit/time changes, status changes and archive. Escaped HTML, selected relationships outside the dashboard's 500-row limit retained.
- **API and data:** owner-authenticated GET `/api/schedule` by viewMode/anchorDate and keyset cursor 50 items per page; writes `schedule_save` / `schedule_archive` via existing guarded POST action, replay receipts and revision conflict checks. Source manual only (`source_kind='manual'`, `auto_source_key=''`). Optional linked object/order must be real; a chosen order determines its canonical object; new links to done/cancelled orders rejected. No Worker master in standalone MVP.
- **Migration:** isolated TEST-only additive `migrations/0004_schedule_manual.sql`; new `mvp_schedule_items` and `mvp_schedule_requests` with index. Not a Google/legacy data import. Original ScheduleItem IDs not reused. Automatic work-order lifecycle synchronization, terminal-order manual-future cleanup and worker assignment belong to a later V9B block.
- **Backup:** authenticated JSON export contains `scheduleItems`, including archive, and rejects more than 500 rather than silently dropping rows. CI rehearses the migration twice on disposable PostgreSQL and checks auth/CRUD/week/month/links/revision/idempotency/archival/paging and old-module regressions.
- **Safe rollback:** deploy previous exact TEST `c03aba331d4b91c3bd9c158d4c9ce2a986abf66a` / `dep-dbl3ev67bikc7389bo9g`; do not remove additional tables or any actual user plans. Android owner acceptance NOT_RUN by owner preference; no PROD/main/Google change.

## V9B — automatic WorkOrder scheduling (TEST-only, 2026-11-04)
- **Canonical source ownership:** manual `mvp_schedule_items` from V9A remains immutable. New additive table `mvp_schedule_order_items` owns exactly one automatic plan per order, keyed by unique `order_id` and `auto_source_key=WORKORDER|<UUID>`. Both appear in one date/week/month API response. Automatic plans are tagged «Из заказа» and cannot be edited as manual plans. The source WorkOrder controls their date/title/status.
- **Lifecycle:** a new order with `planned_on` generates an automatic 09:00–10:00 Asia/Yerevan plan; changing the date/title updates the same auto UUID; open/active → planned, done → completed, cancelled → cancelled; removing the date cancels (does not delete) an existing automatic plan. No auto row is fabricated for a new undated order. Older manual tasks linked to a terminal order remain unchanged, avoiding silent archiving of user data without a separate owner decision.
- **Atomicity/idempotence:** order create/update/status and automatic schedule writes use one PostgreSQL transaction; request-key receipts in `mvp_order_schedule_requests` prevent duplicate mutations and reject reused mismatched keys. Work-start order status update calls the same sync inside its pre-existing transaction. All existing WorkDay/A1/WorkLog/OwnerActivity behavior is preserved and regression tested.
- **Migration:** `migrations/0005_schedule_order_sync.sql` adds two independent tables and a period index only; it does NOT ALTER legacy/manual order structures. Rehearse twice on disposable real PostgreSQL, then official gated TEST migration and data readback before exact Render deployment. Live Neon TEST had 0 WorkOrders and 1 manual ScheduleItem at planning preflight; recheck immediately before applying.
- **Out of scope:** historic Google WorkOrders import, Workers and staffing, auto-archive of user-created manual tasks when order closes, financial/engineering data ownership. No PROD/main release. **Rollback:** deploy prior V9A exact SHA `d6909aaf56c31f63e9b91a0f8eeb717437b73932` / Render `dep-dbldh0oae00c73buoa20`, retaining additive schema and user records.

## V9D — employee attendance and work-order day links (isolated candidate, 2026-11-04)
- Based on accepted legacy `WorkerDayService.js` and `Config.js` but with new standalone UUID namespace. WorkerDay represents a worker's actual arrival/departure for one Asia/Yerevan date, not the owner's WorkDay, paid WorkerTask, WorkLog or payroll. No historical WorkerDays are imported.
- One nonarchived WorkerDay per worker/date, at most one open day per worker across dates; a day can link to more than one existing open/active WorkOrder only with an active V9C worker/order assignment. The same-day second WorkOrder attaches to the existing open shift without changing arrival. A closed day cannot be reopened through normal entry.
- Mobile `Исполнители`: record arrival with order, append a second assigned order, close shift with mandatory reason (because WorkerTasks are not yet present in Clean), revise recorded times/notes with optimistic version, see recent 6 days and current links. Invalid dates/times and overlapping open days are rejected. Unchanged user data preserved. Detailed list available via owner-authenticated cursor-paginated `GET /api/worker-days` (1-50 entries).
- Owner-authenticated actions: `worker_day_arrive`, `worker_day_depart`, `worker_day_correct`; each atomic transaction includes an idempotent request receipt. The lock order follows V9C assignment → worker → order, to prevent closure/deactivation races.
- Additive isolated TEST-only SQL migration `migrations/0007_worker_days.sql` creates three tables plus indexes, unique day/open invariants and FKs. Does not alter existing Workers, WorkOrders, manual/auto schedule or finance data. Existing owner WorkDay and ObjectPresence remain independent. Data export includes workerDays and their WorkOrder links, hard-fails if any state table exceeds 500 instead of silently truncating.
- CI: Node syntax/VM UI plus actual disposable PostgreSQL HTTP E2E, replay SQL twice, auth, dates/locks, idempotent replay/mismatch, relationship retention, correction, export and WorkLog separation.
- **Promotion gate:** source PR alone does NOT approve Neon mutation, Render TEST release, main merge or PROD. Before applying to canonical `vbos_mvp_clean`, obtain a separate explicit owner approval for exact 0007 migration and confirm backup/rollback and verified SHA. Source branch `feature/vbos-clean-v9d-worker-days-20261104`. Owner device acceptance postponed by preference; no fake PASS.
- Safe code rollback: V9C `c682ce4a98cbc50c2d236ca7034d65fc78e73814`, previous Render `dep-dblg0m8u01pc73a46280`. Do not drop migrated tables or saved records. See `migrations/0007_worker_days_ROLLBACK.md`.
- **Limitations:** early departure/zero-work reason is required for every shift until dedicated WorkerTasks are ported; genuine task timers, pay rates, qualifications, worker history import, shift date corrections and partial link removal are separate future blocks. No financial calculations are derived.


## V9E — WorkerTasks: реальная работа исполнителя (TEST candidate, 2026-11-04)
- **CAPABILITY_ID:** VBOS_CLEAN_V9E_WORKER_TASKS; **mode:** IMPLEMENT_FEATURE; **risk:** L3 (worker/day/order links, shifts, write race, time interval). Base accepted Clean TEST V10 `cfc7e2bd4569b7ae02ac5378c24002cec12bb259`. Own feature branch `feature/vbos-clean-v9e-worker-tasks-20261104`. Dedicated target is *existing Clean TEST only after independent release permission*, never PROD or legacy Google.
- **Expected owner result:** in «Исполнители» within an open WorkerDay and assigned WorkOrder, start one task; finish with actual work type, outcome, clock times, quantity/unit (optional), intervention minutes, problem/rework and notes. Review completed tasks. Current Google-owned WorkOrder card remains read-only but may host new tasks. This follows accepted legacy `WorkerTaskService.js` `startWorkerTaskService_`/`finishWorkerTaskService_` core constraints without copying legacy IDs or automatic legacy finance/benchmarks.
- **Canonical source:** own new `public.mvp_worker_tasks` for new WorkerTask events only. WorkerDay remains attendance, WorkLog remains owner work, Payroll and WorkBenchmarks are excluded. Does **not** imply salary, create WorkLogs, change any WorkerDay clock or original WorkOrder status. No old WorkerTasks are migrated; no new user business outcome needs choosing for this minimal scope.
- **Migration/backup:** additive `migrations/0009_worker_tasks.sql` adds task and request receipt tables, FKs, unique active task per worker and indexed date/order queries. No ALTER of existing tables and no old data rewrite. Rehearse twice in disposable real PostgreSQL. Only after explicit canonical Neon TEST migration approval perform a fresh branch backup, apply exact SQL with SHA proof, verify invariants, then exact Render TEST SHA release. Keep prior V10 copy as fallback. See `migrations/0009_worker_tasks_ROLLBACK.md`.
- **Invariant and race:** task starts only with active worker, assigned open/active order, open WorkerDay, and active same-day order link. Start cannot precede arrival or overlap completed tasks. Only one active task per worker; optimistic task revision and request-key/hash dedup. Finish has strict same-day time, positive duration, required type/outcome/management minutes, unit for positive quantity, problem explanation, optional rework bounds. Departure blocked while task active; corrections cannot put arrival after task start or departure before completed task end. No auto change of attendance reason policy.
- **UI/API:** `worker_task_start`/`worker_task_finish` under existing owner-authenticated `/api/action`; owner-authenticated keyset `GET /api/worker-tasks` with limit 1-50; `/api/state` and guarded export include task snapshots (fail closed on >500 rather than silent truncation). New task panel nested in existing worker-day card. No new app tab, finance or planning hotspot.
- **QA:** standard syntax/VM UI tests, actual disposable PostgreSQL E2E with schema twice; task start/finish, wrong worker/assignment/day, two tasks conflict, idempotent replay/mismatched key, validation, worker-day departure/correction guard, paging/export; explicit assertion no owner WorkLog inferred. Host TEST and Android acceptance NOT_RUN until release gate. No Codex/paid API; owner time and HI_V1 captured in PR.
- **Safe rollback:** runtime code exact V10 `cfc7e2bd4569b7ae02ac5378c24002cec12bb259` / Render `dep-dblj2sjncjis73e0k3bg`; **retain the additive WorkerTask schema and all recorded tasks**. Do not delete user data or overwrite any later live work. WorkerTask schema may persist dormant after code rollback. PROD and legacy Google remain independent.


## V9F — WorkBenchmarks Phase 1: observed worker productivity (2026-11-04)
- **CAPABILITY_ID:** VBOS_CLEAN_V9F_WORKER_OBSERVATIONS; **mode:** IMPLEMENT_FEATURE; **risk:** L2 (read-only performance aggregation, interpretation-sensitive). Parent accepted TEST V9E WorkerTasks commit `707996752bf706ec5428b522f0b9b25d2107dadc`; isolated feature branch `feature/vbos-clean-v9f-worker-observations-20261104`. No new background executor/Codex/API charges. No Engineering functionality edits.
- **Source contract:** accepted legacy `WorkBenchmarkService.js` distinguishes `WORKER_HISTORY` versus `OWNER_HISTORY`, keys by worker + normalized work type + result unit, and computes observedAverage as `sum(result) / (sum(minutes)/60)` (not the simple average of individual speeds), with best observed as maximum per-task speed. V9F implements only the worker observation projection from **new Clean completed WorkerTasks**. It does **not** import Google WorkBenchmarks, approved values, STABLE/LEARNING/PROVISIONAL statuses, score, wage, pay rules or owner WorkLog samples; no automatic benchmark persistence.
- **Business result:** on mobile «Исполнители», tap the worker's «Фактическая скорость работы» and request the last 90 calendar days. Show task count, total measured result, total minutes, weighted observed quantity/hour and best recorded task quantity/hour per normalized `task_type + result_unit`. Explicit “observations only / no payroll” and “no samples does not mean zero productivity” notices. Only user-initiated lazy load; cached results invalidated after finishing a new WorkerTask and on logout/password change.
- **Canonical:** `public.mvp_worker_tasks` read-only, `status='completed'`, positive `result_qty` and `duration_minutes`, nonempty `result_unit`, per-worker + date range. The completed outcome type does not by itself make the sample an approved norm. New owner-authenticated GET `/api/worker-benchmarks?workerId=<UUID>&from=YYYY-MM-DD&to=YYYY-MM-DD` returns 90-day defaults or explicit valid 1–366 day range. Query caps qualifying tasks at 500 and groups at 50, then fails **409** rather than misreport partial data. Pagination, indexes and durable benchmark materialization require separate scoped design if the sample size hits a cap.
- **Scope:** `worker-benchmarks.mjs` read/aggregate module, one `server.mjs` authenticated route, `public/workers.js` display/lazy cache and small generic `public/app.js` event/caching hook, README and full CI/E2E tests. **No new SQL, migrations, IDs or write actions**; no existing order/worker/day/task record changes. Engineering calculator, drawing, UFH, finance/payroll and Google are excluded.
- **Verification:** unit weighted-average, grouping, invalid date, fail-closed >500; VM worker UI rendering/escaping and zero state; real disposable PostgreSQL HTTP E2E for authenticated endpoint and 12.5 metres in 90 minutes → 8.333 m/hour, null-result exclusion, no mutation of owner WorkLog; ordinary Clean CI regressions. Actual hosted/Android checks only after source exact release, not inferred from static or CI.
- **Risks/rollback:** the query is bounded by worker/date and 501 returned rows but may scan historical completed tasks without a specialized supporting index; evaluate EXPLAIN with realistic volumes before a production-scale rollout. Status must read `OBSERVED_ONLY`, never approved benchmark or salary basis. Runtime code rollback to exact accepted V9E `707996752bf706ec5428b522f0b9b25d2107dadc` / Render `dep-dbllq3o8v5cc739g4i70`, preserving all task data. No DB rollback. Shared TEST changes require lease/recheck; main and PROD unchanged.

## V9G — WorkBenchmark source evidence and approval gate (2026-11-04)

**CAPABILITY_ID:** VBOS_CLEAN_V9G_BENCHMARK_SOURCE_GATE
**WORKFLOW_MODE:** ECOSYSTEM_PLANNING / DIAGNOSTIC_ONLY on external sources; repository update is documentation-only
**STATUS:** SOURCE_INVENTORY_VERIFIED / BLOCKED_PRODUCT_DECISION / NO_RUNTIME_CHANGE
**BASE_COMMIT:** `409a873267eec1fa116c58c0c863caab488db605`
**OWNER_BRANCH:** `feature/vbos-clean-v9g-benchmark-source-gate-20261104`
**RISK:** L3 for any subsequent imported/persisted normative benchmark, L0 for this documentation-only checkpoint
**EXPECTED USER RESULT:** establish trustworthy source and explicit approval policy for future standards; never silently relabel observed speed as an approved norm.

### Source and data findings
1. **Legacy normative contract** from accepted main `WorkBenchmarkService.js` and `Config.js`: `OWNER_HISTORY` vs `WORKER_HISTORY`; key includes source, worker when WORKER_HISTORY, normalized work type + result unit. Observed speed is `sum(result) * 60 / sum(minutes)`, rounded to 3 decimals; `bestObserved` is a different metric. Default status `PROVISIONAL`; existing `STABLE` preserves a previously saved `benchmarkValue` instead of replacing it with the new observed average. Legacy task selection prefers a valid `OWNER_HISTORY` record over a valid worker-specific record, regardless of whether it is provisional. This selection is **not** proof of owner approval or statistical adequacy.
2. **Google Sheets source inspected READ-ONLY:** current Drive file titled `Working IP APP`, `WorkBenchmarks` tab, bounded `A1:R1000`, 2026-11-04. Exact tab metadata verified before range reading. 22 nonblank benchmark IDs; 22/22 `OWNER_HISTORY`, 22/22 `PROVISIONAL`, 0 `STABLE`, 22 distinct normalized work type/unit group keys. 41 source samples in total; 15 groups contain 1 sample and 19 groups contain fewer than 3. **No worker-history benchmark** or approved stable value observed in this sheet. Numeric parity: 22/22 `benchmarkValue` and `observedAverage` match `round3(totalResult*60/totalMinutes)` (unformatted numeric API values). Formatted values use decimal comma: never parse them through Javascript Number without locale conversion; prefer unformatted native numerical values.
3. **Authority limitation:** the Google WorkBenchmarks tab is a *source/snapshot candidate*, not certified to be the current accepted PostgreSQL WorkBenchmark authority after the legacy WorkLogs API cutover. A separate exact-source provenance/freshness comparison is needed before any reuse. No legacy worker ID or WorkLog ID can be assumed to match Clean UUIDs.
4. **Clean Neon TEST** (`proud-hat-90291191` / `br-crimson-hat-b9kwg7n6` / `vbos_mvp_clean`) confirmed read-only: `mvp_workers=2`, `mvp_worker_tasks=0`, `mvp_work_logs=1`. Clean `mvp_work_logs` stores `title`, `minutes`, `quantity`, `unit`, but **does not store** legacy `workType`, `cleanMinutes`, `performerMode` or `benchmarkPolicy`. A Clean WorkLog row therefore cannot be identified as a canonical legacy OWNER_HISTORY sample or silently converted to a legacy norm. No `mvp_work_benchmarks` table exists.

### Recommended product decision (not yet approved)
- Preserve the 22 historical owner records **only as unapproved reference proposals**, after a separate authoritative-source gate and identity/type/unit mapping. Show source, number of samples, observed average, date/provenance, and explicit `PRELIMINARY`/not-a-norm label. Do not transfer them as STABLE or a payroll basis.
- A standard becomes `APPROVED` only through an explicit owner action on a particular work type and unit with a chosen value, approval timestamp, revision history and later revoke/edit ability. This is a **proposed** policy, not an implemented business rule. Sample-size, outlier handling, task outcome/quality inclusion and effective date remain product decisions. Three samples is merely a low-sample UI caution in V9F.2, not an accepted certification threshold.
- Until approval, V9F/V9F.2 continue to display observed worker rates and period comparison only. No scoring, rank, salary, automatic punishments or profit accounting inferred.

### Scope / non-scope / rollout
- **This block:** source/authority inventory and this Build Packet update only. Files changed: this `README.md`. No TEST API, UI, DB, Google Sheet or PROD mutation. No new copy of the spec.
- **Next IMPLEMENT_FEATURE is BLOCKED until owner's business decision** on whether to (A) approve owner-defined target speeds independently of legacy import, (B) review certified legacy owner observations as proposals and explicitly approve per type/unit, or (C) use observations indefinitely with no approved norm. Recommended B if reliable legacy authority and mapping can be established; otherwise A. No auto-approval.
- **Needed pre-implementation gates:** current canonical WorkBenchmarks source/version (not a stale Sheet), reconciled legacy type/unit identities and history, choice of approval source/policy, additive migration and full TEST backup/rollback if storing approved versions, idempotent approval/revoke, authenticated action and full PostgreSQL E2E; owner acceptance on Android.
- **Rollback of this docs-only block:** revert this README paragraph or close its draft PR; no runtime/data rollback. **Test evidence:** main source inspection, exact Google Sheet metadata/range QA, Neon read-only column/count queries. **NOT_RUN:** live legacy PostgreSQL canonical WorkBenchmark readback, cross-source parity, import QA, runtime E2E for a proposed norm, Android check. **No Codex, no paid API, no PROD or legacy writes.**

The Google `Working IP APP` source remains the writer for all four existing Clean TEST orders mapped by `mvp_legacy_import_map`; 0 Clean-native orders present. Main Clean Neon `proud-hat-90291191/br-crimson-hat-b9kwg7n6/vbos_mvp_clean` contains 22 immutable historic WorkOrder records, 13 assessments, 24 historical FinanceTransactions, 4 Google-owned operational WorkOrders, 1 active worker assignment, zero currently active WorkerTasks or open sessions. Historic FinanceTransaction rows and `mvp_legacy_finance_report` **are not** an authenticated, current per-order Finance ledger for newly created native Clean orders.
- **Exact Legacy rules:** preview blocks completed/cancelled/archived orders and active WorkerTasks; finance may be RECEIVABLE_REMAINS/SETTLED/UNKNOWN based on real earned/received transactions and requires explicit manual `financeChecked=yes` even with remaining receivable. Close accepts only unfinishedObligationsStatus NONE, valid completion date and validated six-factor assessment. Old score `CLIENT_SCORE_V1_100`: 30% paymentBehavior, 20% communicationQuality, 15% scopeChanges, 20% workAgain, 10% acceptance, 5% satisfaction; excludes UNKNOWN and renormalizes weights, risk only when all six factors known. Full close removes active assignments, reconciles BOTH auto and future manual plans, records change receipts, supports dedup and interrupted-close recovery. Clean presently has none of the transactional assessment/close evidence metadata; generic `order_status=done` stays blocked.
- **Source parity finding (read-only):** 13/13 immutable historical ClientAssessments examined, **10 stored scores / 10 match exact formula**, matching scoreVersion and risk. **3 blank stored scores** preserved: two have no known factor, one (`CAS-2026-0001`) has all six factors known but stored score blank. Do **not** silently recompute and overwrite historical blanks. The pure standalone `close-contract-parity.mjs` is deliberately NOT imported by `server.mjs` or PWA; `tests/close-contract-parity.test.mjs` tests synthetic parity/unknowns/errors without accessing PII or changing live records. CI-only diagnostic, not a new close writer or external reference engine.
- **Future target native-only transactional close:** Migration plan must be versioned and additive, retaining `mvp_orders.id`, status enum, legacy import-map lock and existing role grants. Add audited close facts/ClientAssessment with immutable request key, revision/row-lock and idempotent receipt. Use one PostgreSQL transaction to: validate non-Google ownership, current status/revision, active tasks and open sessions, finance checkpoint, manual obligations NONE, date, six-factor assessment; persist assessment and close metadata; remove active worker assignments with related receipts; terminally update auto and future manual ScheduleItems; persist audit and response; COMMIT. On any failure ROLLBACK all writes, including idempotency claim. On retry match same request key+payload to original response; mismatch 409. Include exact rollback/backups and disposable real PostgreSQL race/rollback tests before any shared Clean TEST migration or direct close action. Never change Google WorkOrder status/IDs via Clean.
- **UNRESOLVED BUSINESS DECISION — FINANCE_AUTHORITY (hard gate):** Legacy could explicitly acknowledge finance checked because a real per-order Finance summary existed. Native Clean currently has no analogous ledger; a statement that the order is SETTLED would be invented. Choose one policy **before** enabling `close_work_order`: (A) require verified current per-order Finance ledger/source and persist its auditable snapshot (recommended for financial correctness, but blocks native close until finance integration); or (B) allow owner to explicitly attest an **external/manual** check, persist provenance/timestamp and `PAYMENT_STATUS_UNVERIFIED` (never `SETTLED` without evidence), independent of finance collections (more usable now but weaker automation). Neither option modifies old Google orders or actual historical transactions. This is a business/data authority decision, not a technical default.
- **Execution gate:** `UNRESOLVED_DECISIONS=FINANCE_AUTHORITY`. Stop before writer code, migration, deploying another runtime candidate, main merge, PROD or cutover. ## CRM-19A — Unified Client History from exact existing linked Leads (2026-11-08)
- **CAPABILITY_ID:** VBOS_CLEAN_CRM19A_UNIFIED_CLIENT_HISTORY. **MODE:** IMPLEMENT_FEATURE; **RISK:** L2 (owner-authenticated scoped read and client card UI only, no new DB schema or data writes). **BASE:** `52136187bcec9c8e95e432920b40b11eca052b8a`; **OWNER:** `feature/vbos-clean-crm19-client-unified-history-20261108`; **TEST:** dedicated Clean Render `srv-dbkvfh2jnfac73a31nv0` + disposable real PostgreSQL CI. **EXECUTOR:** this ChatGPT only, Codex disabled, paid API budget 0.

All history read scopes checked via the owner-only `/api/crm/interactions?entityType=Client&entityId=<UUID>&unified=1` query. Existing Lead-only requests and client direct-only endpoint without `unified=1` remain backward compatible.

No bulk import or sourceLeadId migration. **SCHEMA/MIGRATION:** none, read-only operation, no backup/Neon SQL write or Google edits required.

## CRM-21A — direct Client create/update duplicate protection (2026-11-08)

- **CAPABILITY_ID:** `VBOS_CLEAN_CRM21A_CLIENT_DUPLICATE_PHONE_GUARD`. **MODE:** IMPLEMENT_FEATURE. **USER RESULT:** direct Client create/edit detects another non-archived Client with equal normalized phone, blocks accidental duplicate/mis-assignment with 409 and clear existing-client hint. In Clean, **fail closed instead of silent return/rebinding**, consistent with current safer Lead conversion.

Prefer the exact canonical PostgreSQL function `public.mvp_crm_phone_canonical(text)` already created by `0014_crm_lead_conversion_TEST_ONLY.sql`. Reuse existing advisory lock namespace `pg_advisory_xact_lock(151106,hashtext(phone))` used by `crm-lead-conversion.mjs`, with a serializable per-normalized-phone critical section in an explicit short transaction. Canonical lookup `SELECT id,name,phone FROM public.mvp_clients WHERE status<>'archived' ...` never SearchIndex or just UI list. Same own ID excluded on edit. Requests with duplicate reply `409` BEFORE mutation. Existing create `request_key` idempotent replay maintained; existing update revision check remains. UX hint from locally loaded Client list is advisory ONLY, server is final.
- **SCHEMA/MIGRATION:** **NONE**; current phone-canonical function/index/advisory lock are already in deployed TEST. No unique index or automatic re-keying of historic records, no mass data modification. Archived rows never rewritten. No PROD/legacy Google editing.

## Capacity V1F — exact legacy Calendar ConflictGuard v1.10.5 pre-save warnings (2026-11-11)

- **CAPABILITY_ID:** VBOS_CLEAN_CAPACITY_LEGACY_CONFLICT_V1F. **Mode:** IMPLEMENT_FEATURE / L2. **Owner:** Vahram. **Accepted parent:** exact V1E TEST \`4e1e8a33dd94dac5cdd42d7dfbd8e2a772523cff\`, Render service \`srv-dbkvfh2jnfac73a31nv0\` and Neon isolated \`vbos_mvp_clean\`. **Legacy source:** accepted main \`PlanningCapacityCalendarConflictGuard_v1_10_5.html\` SHA(blob) \`2179d7d7223781454ff0e112ca94365de621ede0\`. This is a *behavioral port*, not a product redesign.
- **Business outcome:** Before existing manual Schedule form \`schedule_save\`, verify overlapping *planned* items, ignoring self on edit. Show exact overlap dates/time, first six conflicts and remaining count; offer next gap (08:00–19:00 Yerevan, 15-minute ceil, up to +14 days, skip Sunday) or \`Сохранить всё равно\`. Proposed replacement only fills existing start/end; user must submit again. Explicit override goes back through the original Schedule action; no extra writer.
- **Canonical source:** one authenticated complete read-only \`GET /api/schedule/conflicts?startDate=YYYY-MM-DD&endDate=YYYY-MM-DD\` returning manual + auto WorkOrder + CRM planned ScheduleItems with persisted IDs. Query overlaps the proposed range plus 14-day alternative horizon (not just visible calendar items); max 2000, reject missing/malformed source. Existing status transitions, order automation, CRM, Capacity, and previously accepted hourly next-slot stay unchanged.
- **Legacy semantics:** touching intervals are not conflicts; existing order-linked items excluded on Sunday, and order-linked proposals do not check Sunday; manual personal plans may conflict on Sunday. Multi-day proposed intervals and cross-midnight saved entries are handled by date. Old code's 92-day conflict iteration cap is implemented as an explicit 1–92-day source completeness boundary in Clean; longer input fails closed rather than making a partial safety claim. This is the only documented edge limitation relative to legacy.
- **Affected files:** \`schedule-conflicts.mjs\` and \`public/schedule-conflicts.js/css\` (new), \`server.mjs\` (auth read+asset), \`public/index.html\`, \`public/app.js\` (intercept existing form/override, stale invalidation), \`public/schedule.js\` (non-writing status context), tests \`conflict-guard.test.mjs\`, \`today-ui.mjs\`, \`e2e.mjs\`, and \`package.json\`. No PostgreSQL migration or export/ID/status changes. No Google/legacy/PROD mutations. No Codex or paid API.
- **Risk/verification:** owner-auth 401 and strict date bounds 400; disposable PostgreSQL readback for manual ID and overlap completeness; VM tests for self-exclusion, equal boundaries, overnight, Sunday, nearest gap, XSS escaping, stale form/input, missing source failure, explicit override. Whole Clean syntax/UI/E2E regression CI on final SHA. Android owner acceptance separate, STOP ON FIRST FAIL.
- **Rollback:** redeploy exact accepted V1E \`4e1e8a33dd94dac5cdd42d7dfbd8e2a772523cff\`. No DB rollback, schema changes, new IDs or table write. Do not merge \`main\` or deploy PROD without explicit release.
- **TEST handoff:** shared Render TEST branch binding is not this feature branch. Never use generic \`trigger_deploy\`; only Render UI **Deploy a specific commit** after CI and exact SHA gate. Check \`/api/health\`, LIVE deployed SHA and Android Plan → \`+ Добавить в день\` → set time overlapping another saved planned item → \`Создать пункт плана\`. Confirm alert, **no save before choice**, \`Предложить\` prefill, and explicit \`Сохранить всё равно\` via canonical Save. Record outcome and stop before implementing Shift/Resize or unrelated Calendar flags.

### V1J integration correction — free working-day navigation and past dates (2026-11-11)

The existing V1G `Свободный →` navigator originally counted all zero-plan dates, including Sundays and company/IP review days. In the accepted v1.9.4/v1.9.5 policy these are not actionable free **working** days. V1J now filters from the same read-only calendarDayPolicy to `WORKING_DAY`, `date >= owner today`, and `count === 0`; unknown/missing/partial policy fails closed (no fake free days), while all calendar dates remain clickable for personal/internal tasks. Past dates are subdued, except that exclusive company-day green retains priority. `public/schedule-overlap.js`, `tests/schedule-overlap.test.mjs`, `public/schedule.js`, `public/capacity.css`, `tests/today-ui.mjs` cover the cross-module integration. No change to existing saved Schedule, WorkOrder or CRM state. Android runtime still NOT_RUN pending the one final exact TEST deployment.

- **Data scope:** only allow-listed form fields and synthetic metadata; no credentials, tokens, cookie values or passwords. draftOwnerScope is a namespaced SHA256 of the authenticated owner's normalized login, not of a session cookie. This is single-owner Clean MVP, not a multi-account redesign.
- **Safe replay:** before the first POST persist immutable requestKey + payload locally; freeze the form while result is unknown. On reload the same payload/key are sent to the existing PostgreSQL idempotent writer. A parsed 400/409 rejection releases the form for correction; a missing/ambiguous response preserves the frozen attempt. Confirmed save removes only this owner's exact order/date draft. Explicit deletion of a pending record warns it cannot undo a possible database write.
- **Migration:** none. Browser site-data deletion/incognito/private modes, quota failure, device loss and owner login renaming remain limitations. Other tabs sharing the same key are not a supported collaborative editing mechanism. Do not age out drafts automatically.
- **Acceptance:** no lost entered values, original 09:15 restored, draft survives unavailable server, order/user separation, failed-submit retention, same-key deduplication, and clear only on saved:true.
- **Checks / release:** npm run verify:source, npm test, disposable PostgreSQL npm run test:e2e, exact-source hosted TEST and Android acceptance are required as applicable. Record each as PASS/FAIL/NOT_RUN. No GitHub Actions. STABLE_MAIN prohibits merging an unaccepted candidate into main only to access TEST.

If separately released to TEST later, recover exact prior confirmed Render deploy dep-dbukm1ek1f9s73aiq4r0 / source 04e8bfb221887c3f8c5d9fc594712dc9588f334c with verified readback. Local drafts survive code-only rollback but old UI will not expose them. No PROD approval.

**CAPABILITY_ID:** VBOS_CLEAN_FINANCE_P4_NATIVE_ENTITY_RESULTS. **MODE:** IMPLEMENT_FEATURE with read-only financial aggregation / UI; risk L2 for data interpretation and query performance, no writer changes. **OWNER:** Vahram, «Ок начни» after P3 integrated/accepted. **BASE:** main 3f29ba4470e0b091c8632f014eaa6b8ea0ee81f8 (accepted P3 exact source tree); separate branch feature/finance-p4-native-entity-results-20261123 and Draft PR. **OWNER TEST:** existing Render srv-dbkvfh2jnfac73a31nv0 at https://vbos-clean-mvp-2026.onrender.com/, LIVE source P3 8ad8799196fa377d87afec4d7f312700de5971fe / dep-dc2957bncjis739l9oog. **USER VALUE:** Existing B23 per-order native result and P1 owner-only native scoped summary/history already exist; frozen legacy Object and Worker detail screens also exist but belong to separate Google archive. P4 adds one current-NATIVE cross-entity overview to Finance, with switch between Objects and Workers, text lookup, bounded 20-row keyset pagination and per-entity scoped drill-down to the already implemented P1 native summary/history. Object cards show current native earned, received, economic expenses, profit, cash effects. Worker cards show amounts accrued to worker, paid, net payable and linked business economic expense. Both include active vs archived transaction counts. Zero-new-transaction entities are explicitly excluded; archive never silently merged or remapped by display name. Worker amounts do NOT claim to be complete historical payroll/timesheets or total earnings; only linked new native transactions.

**DEFINITIONAL / AUTHORITY CONTRACT:** Owner-signed B18 POSTGRES / ACTIVE source gate, native finance transaction UUID and immutable original IDs. P4 SELECTs only mvp_finance_native.transactions with CLEAN_NATIVE_FINANCE, links via native object_id or worker_id, joins canonical public.mvp_objects / public.mvp_workers by UUID, returns read-only rows and bounds per response. P4 Object cumulative metrics import the exact P1 accounting SQL expressions (no duplicate independent finance policy). Worker accrued additionally counts only active WORKER_ACCRUAL and WORKER_ACCRUAL_PAYMENT, worker paid/payable reuse P1. Archived transactions contribute to counts but not active money. Distinct entity result pagination uses max native created_at + entity UUID keyset, filter-bound base64url cursor, max 50 server-side, default 20. Text search uses PostgreSQL parameters + escaped LIKE meta; source money stays decimal text, no binary float ledger arithmetic. No Google, legacy, source_id name mapping, cross-object allocations, editing fund policies or cash initial balances. Historical legacy screens preserved below unchanged. Neither source nor UI issues a financial POST.

### SOP continuation — 2026-11-20 PostgreSQL JSONB timestamp replay parity

- **Mode:** `PATCH_FIX` of the already authorized `VBOS_CLEAN_LEGACY_KNOWLEDGE_SOP_COPY_V1` candidate. **Starting checkpoint:** `e4356d5f864c0ad5ffce1a3df0d266a5590ddcfa`. Owner: Vahram. This is not a new feature, source migration approval or release.
- **Verified source defect:** `pg` decodes `timestamptz` columns into JavaScript `Date`; when the same response is stored as JSONB for idempotent request replay, JSON serialization transforms dates into ISO strings. Previously `knowledgeSave` returned raw `Date` values on first save but JSON strings for the identical replay, so native `node:assert/strict.deepEqual(first,replay)` in the prepared PostgreSQL E2E would fail even if SQL succeeded.
- **Bounded correction:** `knowledge.mjs::knowledgeFromRow` now normalizes `createdAt` and `updatedAt` to ISO strings for `Date` inputs, and preserves existing string/null values deterministically. The same canonical response shape applies to reads, first saves, and JSONB replay. Source data and timestamps in DB are not rewritten.
- **Regression lock:** `tests/knowledge-sop.test.mjs` uses real JavaScript `Date` objects in both SQL mocks for read and delayed-save replay and checks canonical ISO dates. Exact new candidate source evaluation in isolated V8 with Node/SQL stubs: **8/8 focused tests PASS**, including earlier legacy timezone year boundary, guarded writer, idempotency, and visual view contract. Separate ordinary Node v22 check confirmed `Date` vs serialized JSON string fails native deep equality. This combination supports diagnosis, **not** a full native `npm test` or real PostgreSQL E2E PASS.
- **Environment:** exact GitHub repo ZIP/checkout still unavailable from local container because DNS fails for `github.com`, `api.github.com` and `codeload.github.com`; local Node v22 and npm exist, but no safe PostgreSQL daemon, Docker or Podman was found. No allowed GitHub Actions authorization for this specific block. Browser connector requires another browser context. **NOT_RUN:** full `npm run verify:source`, full `npm test`, disposable PostgreSQL `npm run test:e2e`, authenticated hosted TEST/Android. Keep this status even though isolated source-level checks PASS.
- **Authority/rollback:** no SQL 0037 applied, no SOP source cards imported, no Google/old Supabase writer switched, no occupied Neon TEST or Render TEST changes, no PROD, no paid services or Codex. Candidate-only revert/close PR is the rollback. Full source migration/identity reconciliation and exact owner-approved release remain STOP gates. No manual files to copy.
